- [Security of systems and networks](/courses/msc-securityofsystemsandnetworks.md): Course description for SSN, covering applied cryptography, security protocols, and network security, including objectives, evaluation criteria, and section details.
- [Security of Systems and Networks](/courses/msc-securitysystemsnetworks.md): Course SNE-08 covering network security, applied cryptography, security protocols, and their real-world applications through theory and projects.
- [Sensing, Perception & Actuation](/courses/msc-sensing-perception-actuation.md): Course covering physical principles of sensors, data analysis, error analysis, image processing, point cloud processing, and sensor fusion for autonomous systems.
- [MSc Technical Electives](/courses/msc-technicalelectives.md): List of Master of Science technical elective courses offered at Innopolis University, categorized by Fall and Spring semesters.
- [Unit-economics for IT startups](/courses/msc-unit-economics-for-it-startups-936.md): A course on metrics-based decision making for IT startups, covering unit economics, cohort analysis, and data-driven product management.
- [Unit-economics For IT startups](/courses/msc-unit-economics-for-it-startups.md): Course covering product metrics, data-driven decision making, unit economics, cohort analysis, and metrics collection automation tools for IT startups.
- [MSc: Unit economics in it business](/courses/msc-unit-economics-in-it-business.md): A course teaching students to collect product data, analyze unit economics using key metrics, and make data-driven decisions for IT business efficiency.
- [Venture and Growth Hacking](/courses/msc-venture-and-growth-hacking.md): Course CSE807 exploring venture capital foundations, investor attraction, deal structuring, and startup financing strategies for entrepreneurs.
- [Venture Capital Hacks in IT industry: From Zero to Negotiating and Investment Deal](/courses/msc-venture-capital-and-growth-hacking.md): Course CSE807 exploring venture capital foundations, investor attraction, deal negotiation, and startup financing for IT industry entrepreneurs.
- [IT Product Development](/courses/mshe-it-product-development.md): Course CSE807 covering the transformation of ideas into MVPs through user research, prototyping, and data-driven iterative development techniques.
- [MSTE: Analysis of Software Artifacts](/courses/mste-analysis-of-software-artifacts.md): Course covering software quality analysis, testing, static analysis, and model checking, culminating in a hackathon project evaluating open source software.
- [CEO Toolkit](/courses/mste-ceo-toolkit.md): A course on practical management for CEOs, covering strategy, operations, finance, HR, legal aspects, IP law, and innovations for technology startups.
- [MSTE: Introduction To Programming](/courses/mste-introduction-to-programming.md): Course CSE101 covering fundamental programming concepts, including structured, object-oriented, generic, and concurrent programming in Java.
- [IT Product Development](/courses/mste-it-course-903.md): Course CSE807 covering the transformation of ideas into MVPs through user research, prototyping, and data-driven iterative development techniques.
- [IT Product Development](/courses/mste-it-course.md): Course syllabus for IT Product Development, covering entrepreneurial steps, market research, agile development, and business model design for tech startups.
- [IT Product Development](/courses/mste-it-product-development.md): Course covering the process of developing an MVP from idea to launch, focusing on customer value, data-driven decisions, and iterative improvement.
- [MSTE: Leadership in entrepreneurship](/courses/mste-leadership-in-entrepreneurship.md): A course exploring the entrepreneur as a leader, focusing on personal branding, team management, and creating positive societal impact in IT entrepreneurship.
- [Market research for it startups](/courses/mste-market-research-for-it-startups.md): Course for entrepreneurs covering methods and guidelines for business research, hypothesis thinking, and assessing market potential for IT startups.
- [IT Product Development](/courses/mste-mste-it-course.md): Course CSE807 covering the transformation of ideas into MVPs through user research, prototyping, and data-driven iterative development techniques.
- [CEO Toolkit: Strategy, Operations, Finance, HR, Legal Aspects, IP Law and Innovations](/courses/mte-ceotoolkit-strategy-operations-finance-hr-legalaspects-iplawandinnovations.md): A course on technology entrepreneurship covering strategy, operations, finance, HR, legal aspects, IP law, and innovations for managing technology companies.
- [Founder Project](/courses/mte-founderproject.md): A course on technical entrepreneurship focusing on startup development, mentorship, and founder skills through workshops and project work.
- [IT Product Development](/courses/mte-itproductdevelopment.md): Course CSE807 covering the transformation of ideas into MVPs through user research, prototyping, and data-driven iterative improvement techniques.
- [Academic Research and Writing Culture](/courses/phd-academicresearchandwritingculture.md): A PhD-level course on academic writing conventions, IMRaD structure, referencing styles (APA/IEEE), plagiarism avoidance, and publication strategies.
- [Physical Culture and Sport Exercise Physiology Mindfulness](/courses/physical-culture-and-sport-exercise-physiology-mindfulness.md): A course on mindfulness and meditation techniques, covering awareness, emotion management, and inner resources for resilience and clarity.
- [Corporate Identity Branding / Communication for Startups](/courses/te-corporateidentitybranding.md): A course on corporate identity and branding for startups, covering communication strategies from bootstrap to global markets.
- [Product-Market Fit](/courses/te-productmarketfit.md): A course covering customer development, product management, and idea validation to achieve product-market fit.

## procedures

- [About lecturing a course](/procedures/about-lecturing-a-course.md): Guidelines for instructors on course syllabus submission, ongoing assessment (OPA), mid-semester evaluation, final interim assessment, and grading policies.
- [Academic Leave](/procedures/academic-leave.md): Describes the basis and procedure for granting academic leave at Innopolis University, including contact details for the Student Support and Development Department.
- [Exchange program](/procedures/exchange-program.md): Guide for applying to Erasmus+ or regular exchange programs, including eligibility, course matching, and partner university information.
- [FAQ for offering an elective](/procedures/faq-for-offering-an-elective.md): Guide for instructors on proposing, hiring, and teaching elective courses at Innopolis University, including content criteria, administrative steps, and student management.
- [FAQ for Offering Elective Courses](/procedures/faqforofferingelective.md): Guide for instructors on proposing, selecting, hiring, and teaching elective courses at Innopolis University, including deadlines, requirements, and administrative details.
- [General Information](/procedures/general-information.md): Guide for instructors on using Moodle, remote teaching tools like Microsoft Teams, and the BAAM attendance tracking system.
- [How To Moodle](/procedures/how-to-moodle.md): Guidelines for professors on setting up and managing courses in Moodle, including syllabus upload, grading, and attendance.
- [Professor Information Page](/procedures/professor-information-page.md): Guide for new faculty on Moodle access, required employment documents, and migration procedures for registration in Russia.
- [Guidelines and FAQ for offering elective courses](/procedures/the-guidelines-faq-for-offering-elective-courses-at-innopolis-university.md): Guidelines for instructors on proposing, selecting, hiring, and teaching elective courses at Innopolis University, including deadlines, student groups, and administrative procedures.
- [Guidelines and FAQ for offering elective courses](/procedures/the-guidelines-faq-for-offering-elective-courses.md): A guide for instructors on proposing, selecting, hiring, and teaching elective courses at Innopolis University, including deadlines, requirements, and administrative details.
- [What if I failed](/procedures/what-if-i-failed.md): Explains the rules for expulsion and reinstatement, providing links to regulations and contact details for the Student Support and Development Department.

- [Technical Electives](/schedules/technical-electives.md): Timetable for technical elective courses including instructors, blocks, and specific dates for the semester.

- [Summer School](/pages/summer-school.md): A list of international summer school programs available to students, categorized by region, including details on fees, deadlines, and application links.

## guides

- [Academic integrity and disciplinary measures](/guides/academic-integrity.md): What counts as a violation of academic integrity at the university, how a case is handled, how a student can appeal, and which disciplinary measures can follow.
- [Academic leave](/guides/academic-leave.md): Academic leave: who can get it and on what grounds, for how long, how to apply, what the decision and the return look like, and what the student must do before and after.
- [Eligibility and Duration Reduction for Accelerated Study](/guides/accelerated-study-eligibility-and-duration.md): Who qualifies for accelerated study under an individual plan and how much shorter will my degree program be?
- [How the BSc programme is organized](/guides/bsc-structure.md): How the BSc programme (Computer Science and Engineering) is organized: duration, tracks and streams, course load by year, internships, theses and language requirements.
- [campus-behavior-dress-code](/guides/campus-behavior.md): Campus Behavior and Dress Code Guidelines
- [Recognition of earlier study results](/guides/credit-transfer.md): How results of earlier study (other universities, certificates) can be recognized as credits at the university: who can apply, how, and on what basis.
- [How to appeal your thesis defense results](/guides/defense-appeal-procedure.md): What is the deadline and procedure for filing an appeal after the defense results are announced?
- [Elective courses](/guides/electives.md): Which elective courses a student can choose, how the lists are organized, and how complete the information about them is.
- [Managing Your Electronic Portfolio](/guides/electronic-portfolio-guide.md): What is the purpose of the electronic portfolio and which sections must I fill out myself?
- [Enrollment Contract and Voluntary Withdrawal](/guides/enrollment-and-withdrawal.md): What must be included in the enrollment contract and what are the procedures for voluntary withdrawal?
- [Exam Accommodations for Disabilities and Health Issues](/guides/exam-accommodations.md): What forms and adjustments are available for ongoing and interim assessments for students with disabilities or serious health conditions?
- [How to Enroll as an Extern](/guides/extern-enrollment-procedure.md): How do I apply to be officially enrolled as an extern to take my exams at the university?
- [Mandatory vs Optional Course Status](/guides/faculty-vs-elective-status.md): Are faculty and elective courses mandatory for students, and how do their requirements differ?
- [Financial Aid, Scholarships, and Grants](/guides/financial-aid-and-scholarships.md): How do I apply for scholarships, grants, and material aid, and what are the eligibility rules?
- [Grading scales across courses](/guides/grading-scales.md): Which grade scales (letter grades and the points for each) the courses use, what is the most common one, how much they differ, and what the university's regulation says about letter grades, passing and the points systems that teachers may use.
- [Final state attestation (graduation)](/guides/graduation.md): How the final state attestation (graduation) works for bachelor and master students: its forms, the commissions, the thesis requirements and appeals.
- [Rules for Retaining or Losing the Scholarship](/guides/grant-retention-loss-rules.md): Under what conditions can a winner lose their grant or be disqualified from receiving it?
- [Индивидуальный учебный план](/guides/individual-study-plan.md): How do I apply for an individual study plan, and what are the rules for its approval, academic schedule, and assessment?
- [Technical Requirements for Remote Assessments](/guides/remote-assessment-technical.md): What computing devices and technical setup do I need to take a remote exam or test?
- [Failing a course and retakes](/guides/retakes.md): What happens when a student fails a course: what the university's regulations say (academic debt, retakes, limits, expulsion, academic leave), who to ask, and what the course pages add about retakes.
- [How university rules affecting students are made and communicated](/guides/student-rights-in-local-acts.md): How are local normative acts that impact student rights adopted and notified to students?
- [Teaching staff and their courses](/guides/teaching-staff.md): Who teaches at the university according to the wiki, how to reach them, and how much of the course list is covered by the staff pages.
- [Перевод с одной образовательной программы на другую](/guides/transfer-between-programs.md): What is the step-by-step procedure and required documentation to transfer from one study program or form of study to another?
- [Moving from paid to state-funded study](/guides/tuition-to-budget.md): How a student can move from paid study to state-funded (budget) study: who is eligible, the conditions, how vacancies are found, how to apply and how the decision is made.
